训练的人更有用，它解释了 LenVM 作为长度 value baseline 和 reward shaping potential 的关系。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses token-level modeling of generation length. Existing methods usually operate through prompts, sequence-level length penalties, or pre-decode predictors. They can constrain the final response only coarsely, and they do not describe how far each intermediate decoding state is from termination.","importance":"The useful idea for pretraining research is to cast length modeling as scalable value pretraining. The signal is annotation-free, does not require a reward model, and provides a target at every generated position. This is not a general-purpose value model, and the paper does not show downstream RL gains. Its contribution is narrower: it shows how a dense token-level auxiliary signal can be derived from rollouts themselves and then used for length control, inference-cost trade-offs, and potentially as a baseline in RL-style training.","technical_approach":"LenVM defines remaining length as a discounted return. Each generated token receives a constant negative reward until EOS, and the value at a decoding state is the discounted sum of future negative rewards. This maps raw remaining lengths, which may range from a few tokens to more than 32k tokens, into a bounded monotone target space. A scalar LenVM head is attached to the LM hidden states and trained to predict this return. Under a fixed rollout policy, the realized return from sampled completions is an unbiased Monte Carlo target for the corresponding value. At inference time, the predicted values can be used for length prediction, length-guided decoding, and exponential tilting of the token distribution to trade response quality for shorter or longer generations. The appendix also frames LenVM as a length-specific value function in PPO-style optimization and as a potential for policy-invariant reward shaping.","experiments":"$21","reading_guide":"A 30 to 45 minute skim is enough. Read Section 3 first, especially the discounted-return formulation and the LenVM head. Then inspect Sections 4.2 and 4.3 to see how the value signal is used during decoding. Section 5 is useful for practical details: target parameterization, gamma, batching, and precision. Appendix C is the most relevant part for researchers thinking about RL pretraining, because it connects LenVM to value baselines and reward shaping.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"不是 pretrain 论文，但把长度控制做成 token-level value head 这件事很干净；只看方法节和 LIFEBench 主表，判断能否接到你们的 decoding stack。","en":"Not a pretraining paper, but the token-level value formulation for length control is unusually clean; read the method and the main LIFEBench table to see if it plugs into your decoding stack."},"metadata":{"institutions":["University of California, Santa Barbara","Carnegie Mellon University","University of Wisconsin–Madison","LMSYS Org","Apple","Apple I","Scalability Axes","Length Value Model: Scalable Value Pretraining for Token-Level Length Modeling"],"authors":["Zhen Zhang","Changyi Yang","Zijie Xia","Zhen Yang","Chengzhi Liu","Zhaotiao Weng","Yepeng Liu","Haobo Chen","Jin Pan","Chenyang Zhao","Yuheng Bu","Alkesh Patel","Zhe Gan","Xin Eric Wang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["length-control","value-model","decoding","inference-time","LIFEBench"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27039","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2604.27039","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2604.27844","title":"ZipCCL: Efficient Lossless Data Compression of Communication Collectives for Accelerating LLM Training","abstract":"Communication has emerged as a critical bottleneck in the distributed training of large language models (LLMs). While numerous approaches have been proposed to reduce communication overhead, the potential of lossless compression has remained largely underexplored since compression and decompression typically consume larger overheads than the benefits of reduced communication traffic. We observe that the communication data, including activations, gradients and parameters, during training often fo","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-30T13:29:59+00:00","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2604_27844.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.DC","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是大规模 LLM 训练中的一个系统瓶颈：分布式 collective 通信越来越贵，尤其在 FSDP、TP、MoE 的 All-to-All / Reduce-Scatter 路径上，网络时间已经压过了算力时间。已有压缩方法多是有损量化或稀疏化，训练风险难控；通用无损压缩又因为压缩/解压开销太大，放不进训练关键路径。作者要回答的是：能不能利用 LLM 训练张量的统计规律，做一个真正能在训练时加速的无损通信库。","importance":"对基座预训练研究员来说，这不是部署优化，而是直接影响训练吞吐、并行扩展性和可承受 batch/模型规模的基础设施工作。它的意义有三层。第一，它把“训练张量近高斯分布”这个常见经验变成了系统设计依据，说明训练动态的统计结构可以反过来指导通信协议。第二，它提供了一个比有损压缩更稳妥的路径：在不改数值结果的前提下减少通信体积，避免重新评估收敛风险。第三，它对 MoE 尤其重要，因为 All-to-All 是 MoE 扩展的硬瓶颈之一。若通信时间能稳定下降 20% 到 30%，端到端训练速度提升 16% 到 18%，这对大规模预训练的总成本是实打实的。","technical_approach":"方法核心不是单一压缩算法，而是把“可压缩性分析 + 编码设计 + GPU kernel + collective 策略”一起做了。\n\n第一部分是 exponent coding。作者观察到 BF16 张量在训练中常接近高斯或对数正态，LayerNorm 和较稳定的优化过程会让数值范围集中，因此 BF16 的指数位分布高度偏斜，少数 exponent 覆盖大多数元素。传统熵编码往往需要在线构建直方图、排序或自适应码表，这在训练关键路径上太慢。ZipCCL 的做法是基于近高斯假设，直接推导 exponent 集中的理论界，预先构造紧凑的 remapping lookup table，绕开动态统计和复杂前处理。这样压缩阶段更像轻量重编码，而不是完整通用压缩流程。\n\n第二部分是 GPU 侧 kernel 设计。作者没有把压缩当成独立算子塞到通信前后，而是按 collective 的数据流重排内存布局，减少额外搬运。实现上重点处理了 global memory 访问、shared memory bank conflict、流水线化的数据搬运与编码重叠，以及 kernel launch 开销。论文明确指出，通用 GPU 压缩器之所以在训练里失效，不是压缩率不够，而是 launch 和数据变换成本把收益吃掉了。\n\n第三部分是面向具体 collective 的自适应策略。对 MoE 的 All-to-All，作者考虑了 expert 负载不均衡带来的消息大小变化和同步拖尾，设计了自适应通信策略。对 Reduce-Scatter，则做了动态切换器，根据系统特征和张量情况决定是否启用压缩路径。这个设计很实际，因为无损压缩并非在所有消息规模和拓扑下都划算。\n\n整体上，ZipCCL 更像 NCCL 的压缩增强版，而不是一个离线 codec。它强调 drop-in replacement，说明作者把工程边界放在“训练系统可直接接入”而不是“理论压缩率最高”。","experiments":"实验在 64 GPU 集群上进行，覆盖 MoE 和 dense 两类模型，文中点名包括 DeepSeek-V3、Qwen-MoE 和 Llama3-8B，并与 Megatron-LM、TorchTitan 等训练系统对比。\n\n几个关键数字值得记住。第一，作者在 Qwen3-A22B MoE 训练的 All-to-All 路径上观察到，原始通信体积约为压缩后体积的 1.33 倍，也就是平均可获得约 25% 左右的数据量缩减，而且这个收益会随训练进行而增加，说明张量分布后期更集中。第二，通信时间最高可降低到原来的约 1/1.35，即最多 1.35× 的通信加速。第三，端到端训练吞吐提升达到 1.16× 到 1.18×。\n\n论文的说服力主要来自三类对比。其一，和直接 NCCL 比，ZipCCL 在通信受限场景下确实能把压缩收益兑现成 wall-clock 改善，而不是只在压缩率上好看。其二，和通用 GPU 无损压缩器如 DietGPU、nvCOMP 比，ZipCCL 避开了“性能悬崖”：后者虽然减少字节数，但总时间反而更慢。其三，作者覆盖了 All-to-All 与 Reduce-Scatter，不只在单一 collective 上报喜。\n\n从预训练角度，更值得看的是 6.6 一类的分布分析章节。作者用实测张量分布支撑近高斯假设，而不是只给系统结果。若这些统计在不同模型、不同训练阶段都稳定成立，那么这种压缩思路有机会成为训练栈的通用组件。","reading_guide":"这篇适合带着系统问题去读。先看 Introduction，明确作者为什么坚持无损而不是量化。然后直接看方法总览图，抓住三件事：指数编码、GPU kernel、collective 自适应策略。\n\n建议重点看四部分。第一，4.1 的 exponent coding。这里是方法成立的理论基础，要看清楚作者如何从近高斯分布推出 exponent 集中性，以及 lookup table 为什么能替代在线 histogram。第二，4.2 的 kernel 设计。这里决定它是不是“能跑”的方案，尤其要看是否避免了额外 transpose、pack/unpack 和 launch 开销。第三，5 节的 All-to-All / Reduce-Scatter 策略，因为 MoE 训练里真正难的是负载不均衡和消息形态变化。第四，实验里的 ablation 和 sensitivity，特别是不同消息大小、不同并行策略、不同训练阶段下压缩收益是否稳定。\n\n如果只花 25 bottleneck in large-scale LLM training: collective communication is increasingly on the critical path, especially for FSDP, tensor parallelism, and MoE All-to-All traffic. Existing communication compression is mostly lossy, which raises convergence-risk questions, while generic lossless compression is usually too slow to help in training. The core problem is whether one can build a lossless compression path that is fast enough to accelerate real training rather than only reducing byte count on paper.","importance":"For foundation-model pretraining, this matters because it directly affects throughput, scaling efficiency, and the practical ceiling for model size and batch size. It also offers a safer alternative to lossy compression: reduce traffic without changing numerical results, so there is no need to re-validate optimization behavior. This is especially relevant for MoE, where All-to-All is one of the main scaling bottlenecks. Current systems suffer from three compounding inefficiencies: (1) the absence of unified KV cache sizing across all attention architectures--particularly multi-head latent attention (MLA), which is unsupported in general-purpose frameworks, resulting in up to 57x memory over-provisioning; (2) confinement of KV cache to a single memory tier (GPU HBM) despi","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-19T21:34:09+00:00","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2604_26968.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AR","cs.AI","cs.DC","cs.PF"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是大规模 LLM 推理里一个越来越硬的系统瓶颈：KV cache 不是简单地“不够大”，而是同时存在三层错配。第一，现有框架对不同 attention 变体的 KV 大小建模不统一，尤其对 MLA 这类压缩 KV 表示的结构缺少原生支持，导致显著的显存预算错误和批大小浪费。第二，主流 serving 系统几乎把 KV cache 视为只能放在 GPU HBM 里的对象，没有把 CPU DRAM、CXL、NVMe、RDMA、并行文件系统这些层级纳入统一管理。第三，缓存淘汰仍以 LRU 这类被动策略为主，没有利用请求复用、系统 prompt 重复、agent workflow 模板化、RoPE 顺序局部性这些可预测结构，因此频繁把后续还会用到的状态驱逐掉，再付出重算代价。","importance":"它对预训练本身没有直接方法贡献，属于推理系统论文，但对基座研究仍有两点现实意义。第一，attention 结构设计不能只看训练时 FLOPs 和质量，也要看 serving 时 KV 形态是否能被系统正确建模和利用。文中把 MHA/GQA/MQA/MLA 放进同一套 sizing 逻辑，提醒人们像 MLA 这样的结构，真正收益只有在 serving 栈跟上后才会兑现。第二，长上下文和 agent 场景正在把“模型参数”瓶颈转成“状态管理”瓶颈。对做长上下文预训练、KV 压缩、latent attention、memory-aware architecture 的研究者，这篇文章提供了一个较完整的系统侧约束图景：如果未来模型继续扩大上下文，单靠更省 KV 的架构不够，还需要预测式分层缓存和更细粒度的 eviction/prefetch 机制配套。","technical_approach":"方法由四部分组成。1）统一的架构感知 KV sizing。作者先把 MHA、GQA、MQA、MLA 的每 token 每层 KV 占用公式统一起来。关键点在 MLA：它不是传统 K/V head 数乘 head dim 的形式，而是 latent 维度加上单独保留的 RoPE 分量，因此若沿用 MHA 公式会严重高估。文中以 DeepSeek-V3 风格 MLA 为例，指出通用框架若按 MHA 预算，可能出现 57 倍级别的过度预留。2）六层内存层级管理。系统把 GPU HBM、CPU DRAM、CXL memory、NVMe+GDS、RDMA fabric、并行文件系统纳入统一层级，并按带宽和延迟特征做放置。这里不是简单 offload，而是把 KV block 视为可迁移对象，根据访问预测和时延预算决定驻留层级。3）Bayesian reuse prediction。作者把 KV block 的复用建模成 Beta 共轭先验下的命中概率估计，并按 16 种 (block-type, transition-type) 组合维护统计量。这样做的原因是不同块类型和状态转移的复用模式差异很大，例如 system prompt block、tool-call block、continuation block 的未来访问概率并不相同。相比纯 LRU，这种方法试图在驱逐前先判断“以后还会不会回来”。4）head-granular eviction 与 RoPE-aware prefetch。驱逐不是整块一刀切，而是跟踪 attention head 级别的重要性，用 EMA 分数估计哪些 head 的缓存更值得保留；同时利用 RoPE 带来的顺序局部性做预取，减少下一步访问 miss。这个设计的含义是：即便 token/block 粒度相同，不同 head 对后续生成的贡献也不一样，细粒度管理可以进一步压缩 miss 率。","experiments":"文中给出的结果以分析投影和系统级评估为主，而不是完整开源实现上的端到端大规模线上实验。几个核心数字值得记住。第一，统一 sizing 后，在同样硬件上可得到最高 7.4 倍的 batch size 提升；这主要来自对 MLA 等结构不再按 MHA 方式错误预留。第二，六层内存体系把单节点有效 KV 容量从约 40GB 扩展到 38TB 以上，目标是在不破坏延迟 SLO 的前提下承接超长上下文和高并发。第三，Bayesian reuse prediction 在作者设定的访问模式下达到 70%–84% 的 cache hit rate，并带来 1.4–2.1 倍的 projected TTFT 改善。第四，head-granular eviction + RoPE-aware prefetch 相比 LRU 进一步降低约 25% miss rate。第五，在 64-GPU H100 集群的分析配置上，系统投影吞吐达到 4150 tokens/s/GPU，成本约 0.43 美元每百万 token；相对文中最强延迟优化基线 TensorRT-LLM，吞吐约 2.0 倍且成本低 30%；相对 FlexGen，吞吐约 6.4 倍，TTFT P99 低 11 倍。需要注意的是，这些数字多为 analytical projections，不应等同于严格可复现的线上实测。","reading_guide":"泛读即可，重点看三处。第一，看引言和背景里对不同 attention 架构 KV 公式的统一推导，尤其 MLA 的 projected throughput 当成已经充分验证的部署结论。预计 20–30 分钟能抓住主要信息；如果只关心预训练研究接口，读 sizing 公式、MLA 讨论和系统约束假设就够了。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a serving-side bottleneck that has become central in large-scale LLM inference: KV cache management is not merely a capacity issue, but a three-way mismatch. First, existing frameworks do not provide unified KV sizing across attention variants, especially for MLA-style compressed KV representations, which leads to severe over-allocation and reduced batch size. Second, most serving stacks effectively treat KV cache as an HBM-only object, leaving CPU DRAM, CXL memory, NVMe, RDMA, and parallel filesystems outside a coherent placement policy. Third, eviction remains largely reactive, typically LRU-like, despite the fact that many reuse patterns are predictable: repeated system prompts, recurring tool-call traces, and sequential locality induced by RoPE.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper, but it matters for foundation-model research in two practical ways. First, attention architecture choices should not be evaluated only by training-time quality and FLOPs; their serving-time KV representation must also be modeled correctly by the runtime stack. The paper makes this explicit by putting MHA, GQA, MQA, and MLA under one sizing framework. Second, long-context and agentic workloads are turning the bottleneck from parameter storage into state management. For researchers working on long-context pretraining, KV compression, latent attention, or memory-aware architectures, the paper is useful because it shows what system support is required for architectural gains to materialize in deployment.","technical_approach":"$25","experiments":"The evaluation is largely analytical and system-level rather than a fully demonstrated production deployment. While natural for prose, such linearization may introduce additional representational burden for tasks whose computation depends directly on explicit 2D structure, because row--column alignment and local neighborhoods are no longer directly expressed in the input. We study this setting, which we refer to as serialization friction, on a small diagnostic testbed of synthetic tasks with explicit 2D structur","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-29T23:58:45+00:00","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2604_27272.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的不是某个具体算法任务，而是一个更基础的表示问题：当任务的计算规则依赖显式二维结构时，把输入强行序列化成 1D token，会不会额外增加模型负担，并成为性能瓶颈。作者把这种负担称为 serialization friction。论文用三个合成任务做诊断：矩阵转置依赖行列对应，Conway’s Game of Life 依赖局部邻域更新，LU 分解依赖二维位置上的消元关系。核心问题不是“模型会不会做这些题”，而是“同一底层内容，如果以文本序列给模型，和以保留二维布局的视觉输入给同一语言骨干，行为会怎样变化”。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发主要在输入表示，而不是任务本身。很多 LLM 工作默认把一切结构化对象都转成文本，再把性能差异归因于推理能力或数据不足。这篇论文提醒了一点：一部分困难可能来自表示失真，而不是计算本身更难。这个结论对表格、文档、网格、棋盘、程序布局、甚至多模态 token 设计都有参考价值。它没有直接提出新的预训练目标，也没有给出可立即迁移的大规模方案，所以影响量级更像诊断性研究，而不是范式级方法论文。但它提供了一个清楚的实验框架：区分“任务难”与“序列化后才难”。","technical_approach":"方法设计很克制。作者没有试图做复杂建模，而是构造一个尽量可控的双通路比较。两条路径共享同一家族语言骨干：一条是 text-only pathway，把二维对象线性化成文本；另一条是 vision-augmented pathway，把完全相同的底层内容渲染成任务忠实的二维布局，再通过视觉编码器接入同一语言模型。控制变量包括任务定义、样本实例、训练/测试划分、提示模板和评测标准，目的是把差异尽量收敛到“结构如何暴露给模型”。\n\n论文的概念抓手是 structural isomorphism：如果输入表示保留了支配任务计算的关系结构，比如行列对齐、局部邻接，那么模型不需要先从分隔符和 token 顺序里重建这些关系。相对地，1D serialization 会把这些关系隐含化，模型必须先恢复布局，再做计算。作者还做了一个有限的 within-visual 对照：在视觉通路内部打乱或破坏二维布局，性能会下降。这个实验不能完全排除视觉编码器、模态预训练差异等混杂因素，但至少支持“二维布局本身有贡献”，而不只是“视觉模型更强”。\n\n和已有表格/文档/VLM 工作相比，这篇论文的区别在于它不把视觉渲染当压缩或上下文扩展手段，而是把它当诊断工具，用来测量序列化带来的摩擦。它也没有声称视觉一定优于文本；作者明确把结论限定在显式二维结构参与计算的任务上。","experiments":"实验部分的重点是趋势而不是绝对分数。三类任务上，视觉路径都稳定强于文本路径，而且随着任务规模增大，差距通常扩大。矩阵转置中，文本路径在更大矩阵上更容易出现系统性的行列错位；Game of Life 中，错误呈现明显的空间结构，说明模型没有稳定恢复局部邻域关系；LU 分解则更能体现二维依赖链条在序列化后变得脆弱。作者特别强调，文本路径的错误不是纯随机噪声，而常常带有空间模式，这正符合 serialization friction 的预期：模型在“恢复结构”这一步已经出现偏差，后续计算自然连带出错。\n\n论文摘要和给出的正文片段没有完整列出所有数值表，因此这里不虚构具体百分点。可以确定的是：1）视觉通路在三个诊断任务上均占优；2）任务尺寸越大，文本通路退化更明显；3）在视觉通路内破坏原始二维布局会带来性能下降。若精读全文，建议重点核对各任务的 size scaling 曲线、错误热图、以及 layout ablation 的具体降幅，因为这些比单一平均准确率更能支撑作者的论点。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction 和 Concept，理解作者到底在比较什么，避免把它误读成“VLM 比 LLM 强”的泛化结论。然后直接看三个任务的实验图，尤其是随尺寸增长的性能曲线和错误可视化，这两类证据最关键。最后看 within-visual 的布局破坏实验，它决定了这篇论文能否从“模态差异观察”往“布局因素诊断”再走一步。\n\n预计阅读时间 20 到 30 分钟。阅读时建议带着两个问题：第一，哪些任务是真的二维原生，哪些只是可以写成二维；第二，如果把这种 friction 迁移到真实预训练场景，最可能落地在哪些对象上，比如表格、文档、GUI、棋盘状态、图像离散 token 排布。论文的价值主要在提出问题和给出干净诊断，不在于方法复杂度。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a representation problem rather than a new task algorithm: when a task is governed by explicit 2D relations, does flattening the input into a 1D token sequence impose extra burden on the model and become a real source of failure? The authors call this serialization friction. They probe it with three synthetic tasks whose computation is natively 2D: matrix transpose, Conway’s Game of Life, and LU decomposition. The central question is not whether a model can solve these tasks in principle, but whether the same underlying instance behaves differently when exposed as serialized text versus a layout-faithful 2D rendering fed into a vision-augmented pathway built on the same language backbone.","importance":"The main value for pretraining research is the framing. Many LLM pipelines assume that any structured object can be safely linearized into text, and then attribute failures to reasoning limits or insufficient data. This paper argues that part of the difficulty may come from representational distortion introduced by serialization itself. That matters for tables, documents, grids, board states, code layout, and even multimodal token design. The contribution is diagnostic rather than a new large-scale pretraining recipe. It does not introduce a new objective or a scalable architecture for foundation models. What it does provide is a clean way to separate “the task is hard” from “the task became harder after flattening.”","technical_approach":"$26","experiments":"$27","reading_guide":"For a quick read, start with the Introduction and Concept sections to understand the exact claim and avoid overreading it as a generic “VLMs beat LLMs” statement. Then go straight to the task-specific figures, especially the performance-vs-size plots and the error visualizations. Those are the strongest pieces of evidence. Finally, read the within-visual layout disruption experiment, since that is where the paper moves from a modality comparison toward a more specific argument about layout preservation.\n\nExpected reading time is about 20 to 30 minutes. Two useful questions to keep in mind: which tasks are truly native 2D computations rather than merely representable in 2D, and where this notion of serialization friction could matter in real pretraining pipelines, such as tables, documents, GUIs, board states, or spatially arranged discrete tokens. The key bottlenecks are no longer modality coverage or latency alone, but the interaction paradigm itself. First, perception and response are still separated into alternating phases, preventing models from incorporating new inputs for timely adjustment during generation. S","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-30T04:05:43+00:00","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2604_27393.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的是实时多模态交互里的两个结构性问题：一是现有系统大多仍是“先感知、再响应”的半双工流水线，模型在生成过程中很难持续吸收新到达的视觉和语音信息；二是交互仍然主要是被动响应式，缺少基于持续环境理解的主动行为。作者的目标不是单纯把语音、图像、视频接进 LLM，而是把输入和输出统一到同一时间轴上，做连续、全双工、可主动触发的 omni-modal 交互。","importance":"从基座研究角度看，这篇论文的价值主要在统一建模和训练接口，而不是单一 benchmark 分数。它把多模态预训练从“离线理解 + 单轮生成”往“流式感知 + 流式生成”推进了一步，尤其强调 token-level continuous connection 和 shared temporal axis，这对未来原生多模态预训练很关键。它也提示一个方向：如果模型最终要服务实时环境，训练目标和数据组织不能只围绕静态样本和回合式对话。相比纯应用型语音助手工作，这篇更接近预训练范式扩展；但它仍偏系统整合，关于数据配比、训练稳定性、对齐损失设计的公开细节如果不足，研究启发会受限。","technical_approach":"核心方法是 Omni-Flow。它不是把多模态输入简单拼接，而是把视觉流、音频流、文本输出、语音输出都对齐到共享时间轴上，让感知和响应在 token 级别并行展开。这样模型在说话时仍能继续“看”和“听”，并根据新上下文调整后续生成。这个设计针对的是传统 turn-based MLLM 的结构缺陷：后者通常把输入编码完，再一次性进入生成阶段，天然不适合 full-duplex interaction。\n\n架构上，MiniCPM-o 4.5 是端到端 omni-modal 模型，包含三部分：流式视觉/音频编码器、LLM backbone、以及语音解码模块。语音侧又分成离散 speech token 的自回归解码器，以及把 speech token 转成波形的 streaming flow-matching decoder。作者强调从多模态编码器到 LLM 再到 speech token decoder 都是可微连接的，总参数约 9B，支持端到端联合优化。这一点比“外接 ASR + LLM + TTS”的级联系统更像统一预训练模型，因为梯度可以跨模态路径传播，模型内部也更容易学到输入输出之间的时间耦合。\n\n视觉编码采用 SigLIP ViT，并结合 LLaVA-UHD 风格的高分辨率切片和 resampler。full-duplex streaming 模式下分辨率上限较低，以换取实时性；传统模式下则允许更高分辨率。语音生成部分的关键不是普通 TTS，而是 time-aligned interleaving speech generation：输出语音 token 的生成要和并发环境上下文保持时间对齐，避免模型在持续交互中“说的是上一拍的世界”。此外，模型支持 multimodal system prompt，包括文本和参考音频，因此可以做 voice cloning 一类能力。这说明作者把语音输出也纳入统一 token 接口，而不是后处理模块。\n\n和常见多模态助手相比，这篇工作的区别在于：1）强调 full-duplex，而不是轮次式交互；2）强调 proactive behavior，而不是只在用户触发后回答；3）强调端到端连续连接，而不是模块拼装。和纯 VLM 相比，它更接近 AudioLM/VALL-E 一侧的“把语音也纳入语言建模接口”，只是这里进一步和视觉流、文本生成统一起来。","experiments":"实验结论主要有三层。第一，视觉语言能力上，9B 规模的 MiniCPM-o 4.5 接近 Gemini 2.5 Flash，并在同尺度开源模型中表现很强。第二，omni-modal 理解和语音生成上，它超过 Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B，同时计算效率更高。第三，系统层面它支持实时全双工交互，并且可以在 full-duplex 模式和传统 turn-based 模式之间切换，这说明训练和推理接口并非只为 demo 场景定制。\n\n从给出的正文片段可以确认的具体信息包括：模型总参数约 9B；可在小于 12GB RAM 的边缘设备运行；视觉输入在 full-duplex 模式下最高 448×448，在传统模式下可到 2240×2240。论文摘要还强调其 OCR、低幻觉、多语言能力得到保留。若全文中有 benchmark 表，建议重点看三类结果：一是流式交互任务相对 turn-based 基线的收益；二是 omni-modal understanding 与 speech generation 的具体指标；三是主动行为相关评测是否有独立设计。因为这篇工作的真正难点不在静态问答，而在连续交互中保持时间一致性、响应质量和计算效率三者平衡。\n\n需要注意的是，当前公开片段更偏系统宣告，缺少足够训练细节与消融数字。若全文提供了 Omni-Flow 与非时间对齐训练、端到端与级联系统、interleaving speech generation 与普通语音解码的对比，这些消融会比总榜单更有研究价值。","reading_guide":"泛读建议先抓三件事。第一，看 Introduction 和架构图，弄清楚作者所谓 full-duplex 到底是 token 级并行、流式缓存，还是只是低延迟轮次交互。第二，看 Omni-Flow 的时间轴对齐方式，以及 speech generation 如何和环境上下文交织，这决定了它是不是一个真正的新交互建模接口。第三，看实验里有没有把主动行为、持续感知、边生成边更新这几件事单独测出来；如果没有，很多结论只能当系统能力展示。\n\n预计阅读时间 30 到 45 分钟。若只挑重点，优先看模型总览图、训练/推理流程图、full-duplex case study、以及与 Qwen3-Omni 或 Gemini Flash Second, interaction is still mostly reactive rather than context-driven and proactive. Plausibility，并进一步细化成 15 个标准。这样做的目的不是增加标签复杂度，而是让偏好数据和 reward 输出有可解释坐标系，便于后续训练时区分“审美问题”和“生成缺陷”。第二层是数据构建。作者先合成约 20K 高质量 prompts，用多个主流 T2V 模型生成候选视频，再让美学专家按 15 条标准做 pairwise preference 标注，最终形成约 19K 训练偏好对，以及约 2.5K 对的 AesVideo-Bench。这里的关键不是数据量本身，而是标注协议：偏好不是泛泛地选“更好”，而是围绕 VA/VF/VP 三个维度做结构化比较。第三层是 reward model 训练。AesRM 基于 VLM，不走传统“编码后接一个分数头”的路线，而是把比较任务写成生成式 next-token prediction，让模型直接输出 pairwise judgment；作者认为这样更贴近底座 VLM 的原始目标，也更能利用其已有的视觉-语言推理能力。训练分三阶段：先用 atomic aesthetics datasets 做能力注入，让模型学会基础审美元素；再用偏好数据做 cold-start instruction tuning；最后用 GRPO 做基于验证奖励的进一步优化。第四层是可解释 CoT 版本。AesRM-Base 直接输出比较结果，AesRM-CoT 额外输出基于 15 条标准的推理。作者发现单一路径生成的 CoT 容易带来 hallucination，于是用 self-consistency 式 CoT 合成：从 teacher 采多条 reasoning trajectory，用一致性指标筛选和集成，再把高质量 CoT 蒸馏给学生。GRPO 阶段还加入 process reward，用学生 CoT 与教师 CoT 的相似度来约束推理过程，而不只看最终偏好判断。","experiments":"从全文可确认的关键规模包括：约 20K prompts、19K 偏好训练对、2.5K benchmark 视频对。模型族分为 AesRM-Base 和 AesRM-CoT，并在多个视频美学 benchmark 上与已有 aesthetic models 比较。作者的主要实验结论不是单点分数，而是三类现象。第一，分层 rubric + 专家偏好数据能显著提升 reward model 在视频美学判断上的一致性和可解释性。第二，CoT 不是天然有益，低质量 CoT 会放大误判；采用 ensemble/self-consistency 生成 CoT 后，AesRM-CoT 的表现和稳定性都更好。第三，process reward 对 CoT 型 reward model 有额外帮助，说明只优化最终偏好标签不足以约束推理质量。应用层面，作者把 AesRM 接到两种后训练策略里：Flow-RWR 和 Pref-GRPO。定性结果显示，生成视频在光照、构图、细节层次上更好。就你关心的研究价值而言，最该看的不是“美学分数提升多少”，而是 reward 设计和 CoT 数据质量控制如何影响后训练信号。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是 25–35 分钟。先看引言里对三层目标的定义：评价标准、偏好数据、reward model，这决定了整篇工作的边界。然后重点看 hierarchical rubric 的具体 15 条标准，以及数据标注协议；这部分决定 reward 是否真的可解释。接着看 AesRM 的训练方式，尤其是为什么作者坚持用 next-token prediction 而不是 regression head。再看 CoT 合成和 process reward，那是方法里最有可迁移性的部分，和文本 judge / RM 训练很接近。实验部分建议优先看三类消融：是否需要 atomic aesthetics pretraining、单路径 CoT 与 self-consistency CoT 的差异、是否加入 process reward。若时间有限，可以略过大量定性案例图，只保留几组能看出 VA/VF/VP 区分度的例子。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper is not mainly about improving raw video fidelity. different component functions of a finite-sum objective incurs different costs. trai","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-30T15:39:09+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2604_28020.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文研究的是非均匀样本成本下的训练优化：当不同样本的梯度计算成本不同，如何在达到目标误差 ϵ 的同时最小化总训练成本。它把这个问题形式化为 Cost-Aware Learning，并把结论落到 LLM RL 训练中的 GRPO：长 reasoning trace 的 policy-gradient 计算更贵，不应该和短样本按同一概率处理。","importance":"这篇对基座研究的价值在于把“token 成本”纳入优化目标，而不是只看 step 数或样本数。预训练和 RL post-training 都有同一个问题：样本长度、梯度信号强弱、计算成本三者不匹配。论文给出一个清晰结论：理想采样不只是偏向大梯度样本，也不只是偏向短样本，而是偏向梯度范数大且成本低的样本。这个原则可以迁移到长文本预训练采样、reasoning RL、curriculum、数据过滤和 annealing 阶段的 batch 构造。贡献量级主要是方法论和理论层面：给出 cost complexity、匹配下界和一个可接入 GRPO 的采样规则；工程上它不改模型架构、不加 value network，主要改变 policy-gradient 样本选择。","technical_approach":"理论部分考虑有限和目标 f(x)=1/n∑f_i(x)，每个 component f_i 有 Lipschitz 常数 G_i 和查询成本 c_i。用概率 p_i 采样样本 i，并用重要性加权保持梯度估计无偏。对一般凸函数，SGD 达到误差 ϵ 的迭代数与梯度估计二阶矩 S(p)=1/n^2∑G_i^2/p_i 成正比，总期望成本与 C(p)S(p)/ϵ^2 成正比，其中 C(p)=∑p_i c_i。最优采样分布来自最小化 C(p)S(p)，得到 p_i ∝ G_i/√c_i。强凸情形有类似形式，总成本随 1/ϵ 而不是 1/ϵ^2 缩放。论文还给出 lower bound，说明该采样准则在这个设定下不是简单启发式，而是接近最优的成本-误差折中。随后扩展到 subset selection：训练前丢弃部分高成本低收益样本，引入可控 bias，换取更低计算成本。LLM 部分把样本成本设为 prompt+response token 数，把梯度强度用实际或代理的 policy-gradient norm 表示，得到 Cost-Aware GRPO。它在候选 rollout 中更倾向选择“高梯度信号、低 token 成本”的样本，并通过重要性校正避免把目标函数改成简单的短回答偏好。","experiments":"理论实验包含合成验证，用来比较 Cost-Aware SGD、均匀采样、只按梯度大小采样等策略；核心现象是，当 G_i 和 c_i 不一致时，p_i ∝ G_i/√c_i 能以更低总成本达到同样误差。LLM 实验使用 Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B-Instruct 和 Qwen3-8B Base，把方法接入 GRPO，并在多个 reasoning benchmark 上评估。主要结果是，在保持最终性能基本不下降的情况下，policy optimization 的 token/FLOP 消耗下降。给出的内容未包含各 benchmark 的精确分数和节省比例，因此具体幅度需要以实验表格为准。更值得看的是消融：只偏向短序列会损失训练信号，只偏向大梯度会忽略成本；二者的比值式权衡才对应理论推导。","reading_guide":"建议精读，预计 1–1.5 小时。先读 Problem Formulation 和 Cost-Aware SGD，重点看 S(p)、C(p) 以及 p_i ∝ G_i/√c_i 的推导，这是全文的核心。再看 lower bound，确认算法的成本复杂度是否与下界匹配。subset selection 部分适合从数据过滤角度读，注意它如何控制 bias。LLM 部分重点看 Cost-Aware GRPO 的采样单位、梯度范数代理、重要性权重和是否改变 GRPO objective。实验表格需要重点核对两个问题：同等性能下 token/FLOP 省了多少，以及长回答或复杂题是否被过度下采样。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies training when samples have different computational costs. The goal is not only to reach an error target ϵ, but to do so with minimum total cost. The motivating LLM case is GRPO-style policy optimization, where long reasoning traces are more expensive than short ones.","importance":"The main contribution is a clean cost-aware view of sampling. Standard SGD analysis counts iterations, implicitly treating all samples as equally expensive. That assumption is weak for foundation-model training, where token length directly changes FLOPs. The paper shows that the right sampling rule should balance signal and cost: prefer samples with larger gradient norms and lower evaluation cost. This principle is directly relevant to RL post-training, long-sequence pretraining, curriculum design, and batch construction under fixed token budgets.","technical_approach":"The finite-sum objective is f(x)=1/n∑f_i(x). Each component has a Lipschitz constant G_i and a query cost c_i. The algorithm samples item i with probability p_i and uses importance weighting to keep the stochastic gradient unbiased. For convex objectives, the second moment of the estimator is S(p)=1/n^2∑G_i^2/p_i and the expected per-step cost is C(p)=∑p_i c_i. The total cost to reach error ϵ scales with C(p)S(p)/ϵ^2, so the optimal distribution minimizes this product. The resulting rule is p_i ∝ G_i/√c_i. The strongly convex case has the same sampling structure with 1/ϵ cost scaling. The paper also gives a lower bound and a subset-selection variant that trades a controlled amount of bias for lower cost. In Cost-Aware GRPO, the cost is the prompt-plus-response token count, while the signal term is the policy-gradient norm or its proxy. Candidate rollouts are sampled toward high-signal, low-cost examples, with importance correction to avoid simply training a short-answer-biased objective.","experiments":"Synthetic experiments validate the theory by comparing cost-aware sampling with uniform sampling and signal-only sampling. The expected pattern is confirmed: when gradient magnitude and cost are mismatched, p_i ∝ G_i/√c_i reaches the same error with lower total cost. The LLM experiments use Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B-Instruct and Qwen3-8B Base, applying the method to GRPO and evaluating on reasoning benchmarks. The reported conclusion is that token and FLOP usage during policy optimization are reduced without a meaningful loss in final benchmark performance. The supplied content does not include the exact table values, so the numerical savings and per-benchmark scores need to be checked in the experimental section.","reading_guide":"Read this one carefully. Start with the formulation and the derivation of C(p)S(p); that is the central idea. Then check the optimal distribution p_i ∝ G_i/√c_i and the lower bound to see why the rule is not just a heuristic. The subset-selection section is useful for data-filtering and curriculum questions. For the GRPO application, focus on the sampling unit, the gradient-norm proxy, the importance weights, and any safeguards against removing long but useful reasoning traces. The most important empirical checks are token/FLOP savings at matched performance and whether hard problems with longer traces are under-sampled.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"Google将理论优化与大模型RL落地结合的典范，Cost-Aware GRPO直击长序列RL训练的痛点，能省30% token，做RLHF的同学必读其采样和加权策略。","en":"A prime example of combining theoretical optimization with LLM RL from Google. Cost-Aware GRPO directly addresses the pain point of long-sequence RL training, saving 30% tokens. A must-read for RLHF practitioners on its sampling and weighting strategies."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Google Research","Tel Aviv University"],"authors":["Clara Mohri","Amir Globerson","Haim Kaplan","Tomer Koren","Yishay Mansour"],"notable_authors":["Tomer Koren","Yishay Mansour","Amir Globerson"],"tags":["Cost-Aware Learning","GRPO","RLHF","Optimization"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28020","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2604.28020","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2604.27495","title":"Debiasing Reward Models via Causally Motivated Inference-Time Intervention","abstract":"Reward models (RMs) play a central role in aligning large language models (LLMs) with human preferences. n","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-30T15:23:40+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2604_27998.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文要解决的是 latent reasoning 的 RL 稳定性问题。已有 Latent-SFT 能把中间推理压到连续 latent token 中，缩短推理链，但把 GRPO 直接搬到 latent 空间会失效：连续探索容易离开有效 latent 流形，Gumbel 扰动下的概率更新方向可能和轨迹优势不一致，多条正确路径的 latent 平均还可能落到不能继续正确推理的位置。","importance":"这篇工作的启发主要在 pretrain/posttrain 接口和新型语言建模训练目标上。它没有提出新的基座预训练目标，但清楚指出 latent token 不是普通 token 的平滑版本，RL 优化时会改变动作空间、概率密度估计和采样机制。对做 latent reasoning、soft token LM、any-order 或 diffusion-like LM 的研究者来说，关键价值在于：连续动作空间的探索需要先有有效流形，不能指望 RL 从显式 CoT 模型中自动长出稳定 latent 推理结构。","technical_approach":"方法建立在 vocabulary-superposition latent token 上，即每一步用 top-K 词表分布的线性组合表示 latent token，而不是采样一个离散词。作者先用 Latent-SFT 初始化，确保模型已经学到可用的 latent 推理轨迹。随后改造 GRPO。第一，Invalid Sample Advantage Masking 丢弃超长或不终止的 rollout，把这些样本视为离开有效 latent 流形后的异常轨迹，避免它们污染优势估计。第二，One-sided Noise Sampling 把 Gumbel 扰动限制为正向扰动，减少正优势轨迹中某些 latent 分量被压低、负优势轨迹中某些分量被抬高的问题。第三，Optimal Correct Path First Token Selection 针对同一 prefix 下多条正确路径的 mode averaging 问题，只选择轨迹级 surrogate log-probability 最优的正确路径来更新首个 latent token，同时保留后续 token 的其他正确路径更新。这个设计把最容易发生 latent 混合失真的位置限制在一个明确选择上，而不是把多个正确首步平均成一个未必有效的连续状态。","experiments":"实验覆盖 LLaMA-3.2-1B-Instruct 和 Qwen2.5-Math-7B，任务包含低难度和高难度数学推理。低难度设置下，Latent-GRPO 相比 Latent-SFT 推理能力可能需要在 SFT 或 mid-training 阶段注入，单靠后期 RL 不稳定。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies why reinforcement learning for latent reasoning is unstable. Latent-SFT can compress intermediate reasoning into continuous latent tokens, but directly applying GRPO to this space breaks several assumptions of token-level RL: exploration can leave the valid latent manifold, Gumbel-based density updates can disagree with trajectory-level advantages, and averaging several correct latent paths can create a state that supports no correct continuation.","importance":"The main value is for researchers working on latent reasoning and non-standard language modeling objectives. The paper makes a useful point: a latent token is not just a softened discrete token. Once reasoning moves into a continuous action space, the sampling process, density estimation, and optimization geometry all change. This matters for latent reasoning, soft-token LMs, masked/any-order LMs, and diffusion-like language models where training stability depends on whether the model stays on a valid representation manifold.","technical_approach":"The method uses vocabulary-superposition latent tokens, where each latent action is represented as a linear combination over the top-K vocabulary candidates. The model is first initialized from Latent-SFT, which provides a valid latent reasoning manifold before RL. The proposed Latent-GRPO then adds three targeted changes. Invalid Sample Advantage Masking removes over-length or non-terminating rollouts, which are treated as likely off-manifold samples. One-sided Noise Sampling restricts Gumbel perturbations to be positive, reducing cases where components from positive-advantage trajectories are pushed down or components from negative-advantage trajectories are pushed up. Optimal Correct Path First Token Selection addresses mode averaging at the first latent step under a shared prefix: among correct trajectories, it selects the one with the best trajectory-level surrogate log probability for the first-token update, while keeping later-token updates from other correct paths active.","experiments":"The experiments use LLaMA-3.2-1B-Instruct and Qwen2.5-Math-7B on mathematical reasoning tasks. On lower-difficulty tasks, Latent-GRPO improves over Latent-SFT by 7.86 Pass@1 points on average while using a reasoning chain 4.44 times shorter than explicit GRPO. On harder tasks, it improves over Latent-SFT by 14.77 points on average, exceeds explicit GRPO by more than 4 Pass@1 points, and uses a chain 3.31 times shorter. It reports the best Pass@1 among compared methods on Math500, AIME24, and AIME25. With Gumbel sampling, pass@64 on AIME benchmarks exceeds 50. The ablations are important because each proposed component maps to a specific failure mode: missing latent structure, advantage-noise mismatch, and harmful mixing across correct paths.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 35–45 minutes. In the experiments, prioritize hard math tasks, reasoning-chain length, and ablations. While online reinforcement learning (RL) has been introduced t","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-30T17:59:52+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2604_28192.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.RO","cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的是 VLA 中一个很具体但重要的缺口：已有带 reasoning 的机器人策略，要么依赖显式语言推理，延迟高且难表达连续物理过程；要么做 latent reasoning，但训练方式仍主要是 imitation learning，缺少在线交互带来的适应性。与此同时，已有 VLA 的 RL post-training 又大多只优化 action space，没有把“先形成物理推理，再执行动作”这一内部过程纳入优化目标。作者的问题设定因此很明确：能否让 RL 直接作用于 latent physical reasoning 与 action generation 的耦合过程，而不是只在动作层面做 policy improvement。","importance":"它对纯 LLM 预训练的直接价值有限，因为主要是机器人 VLA post-training；但对“latent reasoning 是否能被训练信号显式塑形”这个更一般的问题有启发。尤其是两点。第一，作者把 latent CoT 当作隐式决策变量，而不是中间副产物，这和近来语言模型里对 latent reasoning、hidden scratchpad、continuous thought 的兴趣是同一方向。第二，LAPO 的核心思想是把环境奖励通过联合 likelihood ratio 传回 reasoning token 与 action token，这提供了一种 credit assignment 视角：如果中间推理不可见，仍然可以通过策略梯度间接塑造它。对研究 RL-pretrain、reasoning token、或 post-train 反哺内部表征的人，这个思路值得记一下。","technical_approach":"方法由三部分组成。第一部分是 LaST-R1 架构。模型以预训练 Qwen3-VL-4B 为初始化，在机器人数据上继续预训练。执行时先自回归地产生 latent reasoning tokens，再以这些 latent token 为条件并行解码动作。这里 latent token 不是语言解释，而是连续或离散化后的内部推理表征，用来编码物理动态、未来状态和操作意图。作者还用 vision foundation model 的 global future representations 去 anchor 这些 latent token，目的是给 latent reasoning 一个稳定的语义/空间先验，避免它在训练中退化成无意义隐变量。第二部分是 LAPO。与只对动作概率做 PPO 式优化不同，LAPO 把 latent reasoning 和 action generation 放进统一的 step-level likelihood ratio 中，让环境奖励同时影响两者。直观上，策略不只是学“哪个动作回报高”，还学“哪种内部物理表征更有助于产生高回报动作”。这解决的是 credit assignment：奖励来自环境终局，但更新要能传到中间 reasoning。第三部分是 adaptive latent CoT。作者不固定 reasoning horizon，而是让模型根据任务复杂度动态调整 latent reasoning 长度。复杂任务多想几步，反应型任务少想甚至近似跳过，以平衡性能和时延。训练流程上，先做大规模机器人预训练，再对具体任务做 SFT warm-up，最后进入在线 RL post-training。","experiments":"从引言可确认的关键结果包括：模型以 Qwen3-VL-4B 为底座，在多个机器人操作数据集上预训练；在 LIBERO benchmark 上，one-shot warm-up 后平均成功率达到 99.8%，高于已有基线；在四个真实世界复杂任务上，LAPO 相比 warm-up 初始策略最高带来 44% 提升，单臂和双臂场景平均成功率达到 的收益，说明固定推理长度不是最优折中。第三，真实机器人实验说明改进不只存在于仿真。由于给出的全文片段主要是引言，具体消融数字、训练步数、rollout 配置、奖励设计细节还需要看实验章节原文才能完整确认；但从问题设定和结果摘要看，作者想证明的核心命题已经很清楚：latent reasoning 不是只能靠 imitation 学，也可以被在线 RL 直接塑形。","reading_guide":"泛读建议 30–40 分钟。先看 Figure 1 对比 action-only RL 与 reasoning-before-acting RL 的差异，这是整篇工作的主轴。然后重点读方法部分里 LaST-R1 的生成顺序：latent token 如何产生、如何条件化动作解码、future representation 如何做 anchor。接着读 horizon。真实机器人结果也建议看，因为它决定这类方法是不是只在仿真里成立。若时间有限，可以先跳过具体任务描述和视觉展示，保留算法公式、训练流程图、主表和关键消融表。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific gap in VLA training. Existing reasoning-enabled robot policies either rely on explicit language reasoning, which is slow and poorly matched to continuous physical dynamics, or use latent reasoning but still train mostly with imitation learning, limiting adaptation. Meanwhile, online RL for VLA usually optimizes only the action space and ignores the internal reasoning process. The central question here is whether RL can optimize the coupled process of latent physical reasoning and action generation, rather than improving actions alone.","importance":"Its direct relevance to core LLM pretraining is modest, since this is primarily a robotics VLA post-training paper. That connects naturally to current interest in hidden scratchpads, continuous thought, and latent reasoning in language models. Second, LAPO offers a concrete credit-assignment mechanism: even when intermediate reasoning is not externally supervised, environmental reward can still shape it through a joint policy objective over reasoning and action tokens. 在多次求解中的结果分布来给优势，目标不是生成最容易或最难的问题，而是生成位于 Solver 能力边界附近、能提供高学习信号的问题。\n\n第二块是 iterative self-distilled SFT。作者认为如果直接让 proposer 在稀疏奖励下探索，很容易偏离 valid-output manifold，于是先用自蒸馏 SFT 把模型投影回“能稳定生成合法 specification/solution”的区域，再进入 RL。这一步在文中不是预热细节，而是稳定训练的必要条件。\n\n第三块是 UCB-guided Curriculum DAG。新题目不是无约束加入池子，而是只有通过严格过滤、novelty 检查、并被 Solver 验证后，才作为新节点进入 DAG。UCB 用来在已有 seed 上平衡探索与利用，逐步扩展课程图，避免 curriculum poisoning。\n\n与常见 self-improvement 方法相比，这篇文章的区别不在于 verifier 或环境，而在于把 proposer 学习、solver 学习、valid-manifold 维护和课程扩展机制绑成一个闭环。","experiments":"实验在 Verus 形式化验证环境上进行，底层模型是 To overcome this, we propose a general game-theoretical framework that lifts the problem to a \\emph{Distribu","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-29T20:15:37+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2604_27166.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.GT"],"summary":{"core_problem":"答案级微调（ALFT）需要优化模型生成正确最终答案的边际概率，但推理轨迹到答案的映射通常不可微，且推理轨迹的组合空间过大，直接计算边际概率梯度不可行，现有REINFORCE类梯度估计器方差过高，训练难以稳定。","importance":"为答案级对齐提供了统一的博弈论框架，将此前多种启发式推理对齐方法（如逆频率奖励、共识自改进）统一到凸对偶理论下，无需人工设计奖励函数即可导出可扩展的训练算法，为RL对齐、推理增强预训练提供了理论支撑和落地路径。","technical_approach":"将答案级微调建模为双人分布对齐博弈，其中策略模块是生成推理轨迹的模型，目标模块是辅助变分分布，二者构成极小极大博弈，其纳什均衡等价于原ALFT问题的最优解。基于组相对策略优化（GRPO）导出可扩展的训练算法，包括面向离散答案域的Coherence-GRPO和面向开放生成域的Pairwise-GRPO，直接从同输入的多组输出统计量计算优势信号，无需显式边际化推理轨迹空间。","experiments":"在3个3B参数级指令微调模型（Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct、Phi-3-mini-4k-Instruct、Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct）上测试：GSM8K数学数据集上准确率绝对提升3.18~9.18个百分点（相对提升4.80%~12.46%）；TriviaQA问答数据集上Pairwise-GRPO的精确匹配相对提升最高42.06%，F1值相对提升18.12%。","reading_guide":"泛读推荐重点看博弈框架的核心推导、GRPO变体的实现逻辑、核心实验结果，预计阅读时间20分钟，适合从事模型对齐、推理增强预训练的团队参考。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Answer-level fine-tuning (ALFT) requires optimizing the marginal probability of the model generating correct final answers. However, the mapping from reasoning traces to answers is usually non-differentiable, and the combinatorial space of reasoning traces is too large to directly compute gradients of marginal probability. Existing REINFORCE-style gradient estimators suffer from excessively high variance, making training unstable.","importance":"This work provides a unified game-theoretic framework for answer-level alignment, unifying multiple previous heuristic reasoning alignment methods (such as inverse frequency reward, consensus-based self-improvement) under convex duality theory. Scalable training algorithms can be derived without manual reward engineering, offering theoretical support and implementation paths for RL alignment and reasoning-enhanced pretraining.","technical_approach":"ALFT is formulated as a two-player distributional alignment game, where the policy is the trace-generating model and the target is an auxiliary variational distribution. The min-max game's Nash Equivalence is equivalent to the optimal solution of the original ALFT problem. Scalable training algorithms are derived based on Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), including Coherence-GRPO for discrete answer domains and Pairwise-GRPO for open-ended generation domains. Advantage signals are computed directly from statistics of multiple outputs for the same input, without explicit marginalization over the reasoning trace space.","experiments":"Tested on three 3B-parameter instruction-tuned models (Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct, Phi-3-mini-4k-Instruct, Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct): on GSM8K math dataset, accuracy gains 3.18~9.18 absolute percentage points (4.80%~12.46% relative improvement); on TriviaQA QA dataset, Pairwise-GRPO achieves up to 42.06% relative improvement in exact match and 18.12% relative improvement in F1 score.","reading_guide":"For skimming, focus on the core derivation of the game framework, implementation logic of GRPO variants, and key experimental results. Three approaches have been widely adopted: (i) Proximal policy optimization and advantage actor-critic rely on a deep neural network to estimate the value function of the learning policy in order to reduce the variance of the policy gradient. However, estimating and maintaining such a value network incurs substantial computational and memory overhead. (","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-30T15:27:34+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2604_28005.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","stat.ML"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文解决的是低算力 但依赖较大的组内采样数。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发主要在 Pretrain ↔ Posttrain 接口。它把 value estimation 从“再训练一个神经网络”或“靠大量 rollout 平均”改成非参数估计问题，用较轻的统计估计器换取更稳定的 RL 信号。这个方向适合资源受限的 reasoning RL，也适合思考更一般的训练信号复用：相近 prompt 或相近轨迹上的 reward 信息不一定只能在同一组内平均，可以通过核平滑在局部邻域共享。","technical_approach":"方法是 Kernelized Advantage Estimation。论文用 Nadaraya-Watson kernel smoothing 估计 value function，再用 reward 减去该估计值得到 advantage。与 PPO/A2C 的区别是，它不引入参数化 value network，因此没有额外反向传播和显存占用。与 GRPO 的区别是，它不只使用同一 prompt 下的样本均值，而是利用样本之间的相似性做局部加权平均；这样在每个 prompt rollout 数较少时，仍能降低 value 估计误差。理论部分围绕三个层面展开：value estimator 的 MSE、gradient estimator 的 MSE、最终 learned policy 的 performance，并证明在合适条件下可以接近 oracle value function 带来的效果。这里的 oracle property 是指估计出来的 value baseline 在测试表现上接近已知真实 value 的理想算法，而不是说方法知道真实 value。","experiments":"实验按三个问题验证：value estimation、gradient estimation、policy optimization。论文在 Table 1 比较 value 估计误差，在 Table 2 比较梯度估计误差，在 Tables 3 和 4 以及 Figure 4 比较策略优化效果。结论是 KAE 相比 REINFORCE 和 GRPO 在小 rollout 预算下更稳，性能接近使用真实 value function 的 oracle baseline。消融实验显示收益主要来自更好的 value 估计，而不是其它训练细节；Figure 3 的敏感性分析显示带宽和 kernel 函数选择不会让 MSE 产生过强不稳定性。给出的内容未包含具体模型规模、数据集和表格数值，精读时需要回到实验表格核对幅度。","reading_guide":"泛读建议 30–45 分钟。先看 Introduction 中对 PPO/A2C、GRPO、REINFORCE 的成本和方差比较，确认论文设定是否贴近自己的 RL 训练预算。然后看方法部分的 kernel value estimator 定义，重点关注样本相似度如何构造、带宽如何选、是否需要额外 embedding 或特征。理论部分不必逐行推导，但要看 Theorem 1–3 的条件，尤其是 oracle property 依赖的平滑性和样本覆盖假设。实验部分重点看 Table 1、Table 2、Figure 4 和带宽敏感性图，判断该方法在少量 rollout 下的收益是否稳定。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets advantage estimation for RL-based LLM reasoning under a tight compute budget. A separate value model is too expensive, while GRPO needs many sampled traces per prompt to form a reliable baseline. With only a few rollouts, the policy-gradient estimator becomes noisy.","importance":"The useful signal for pretraining researchers is not the specific benchmark result, but the framing: value estimation can be treated as a lightweight statistical estimation problem rather than as another neural network or a large within-prompt average. This is relevant to the pretrain–posttrain interface, because RL signal quality and compute allocation directly affect how reasoning behaviors are shaped after pretraining.","technical_approach":"The method, Kernelized Advantage Estimation, estimates the value function with Nadaraya-Watson kernel smoothing and uses the resulting baseline to compute advantages. Unlike PPO or A2C, it does not train a parametric critic. Unlike GRPO, it does not rely only on the mean reward within a prompt group; it shares reward information across locally similar samples through kernel weights. The theoretical analysis studies value-estimation MSE, gradient-estimation MSE, and learned-policy performance. Under the stated smoothness and coverage assumptions, the estimator has an oracle-style property: its behavior is close to an ideal method that has access to the true value function.","experiments":"The experiments are organized around three claims: better value estimates, lower-noise gradient estimates, and improved policy optimization under limited sampling. Table 1 reports value-estimation error, Table 2 reports gradient-estimation error, and Tables 3–4 plus Figure 4 evaluate policy learning. Ablations attribute the gains to improved value estimation. The bandwidth and kernel-function sensitivity study in Figure 3 suggests that the estimator is not overly fragile to these choices. it often requires uncovering hidden structure from data. From estimating reaction constants in chemistry to inferring demand elasticities in economics, this latent structure recovery is what distinguishes scientific reasoning from curve fitting. Large language models (LLMs) can often recall and apply relevant scientific formulas, but we show that this ability is surprisingly easy to suppress. We show that adding in-context example","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-30T07:46:55+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2604_27540.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文研究一个在科学类 ICL 场景里很容易被忽略的问题：当任务需要“从观测数据中恢复隐变量/参数，再用已知科学定律计算目标量”（latent structure recovery）时，加入与真实定律完全一致的 in-context examples，反而会抑制模型调用预训练中已掌握的科学公式与推导能力，使其从“知识驱动的推导”转向“基于示例的经验拟合”。作者关心的不是平均准确率升降，而是模型内部解题策略被提示词结构系统性改变。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接启发在于：ICL 并不只是“给更多信息”，它会改变模型在推理时选择的计算路径（检索+推导 vs. 条件拟合）。这意味着很多用 k-shot/CoT 来评估“模型是否学会了某个科学知识”的实验，可能测到的是“模型是否会在给定示例分布下做函数拟合”，而不是“是否能从参数化定律中做结构化推导”。对预训练与后训练接口也有含义：如果 RLHF/指令调优强化了对用户上下文的顺从与模式匹配，可能进一步放大这种策略迁移，从而在需要调用硬知识的场景里出现‘知识被挤出’的现象。更工程化的影响是：科学/工程领域的 LLM 系统默认加示例未必稳妥，可能需要显式设计提示来触发公式检索与符号化推导，而不是让模型走最近邻式的经验回归。","technical_approach":"作者提出“策略竞争（strategy competition）”框架来解释现象。对 latent structure recovery 任务，模型可走两条路：\n1) 知识驱动推导 S_know：由语义线索触发，从预训练记忆中检索领域方程/定律，先做隐变量提取 g(X)->Θ，再做目标计算 h(Θ)->Y；\n2) 示例驱动拟合 S_emp：把任务当作从 X 到 Y 的黑箱映射 f(X)，利用 k-shot 示例在上下文中做条件拟合/模式匹配，绕开显式公式与中间参数。\n核心主张不是“示例会与知识融合”，而是“示例会改变两种策略的权重”，系统性地把概率质量从 S_know 推向 S_emp，即 knowledge displacement。\n为让这种位移可观测，作者刻意选取一类结构：输入是原始观测（时间序列、实验读数、市场数据等），输出需要先恢复参数再计算；并覆盖多个学科的经典方程（文中举例包括 Newton 冷却定律、Nernst 方程、Cobb–Douglas、Snell、Darcy 等）。同时他们强调仅看 end-to-end accuracy 会把机制遮蔽：因为 S_emp 有时比 S_know 更差（准确率下降），有时在噪声/格式上更鲁棒（准确率上升），有时两者接近（准确率不变），但“策略迁移”在三种情形下都发生。","experiments":"实验围绕一个 difficulty-balanced 的 60 任务基准（5 个领域：Biology/Chemistry/Economics/Physics/Geoscience，各 12 个任务），并在 4 种模型架构上做约 6,000 次试验。任务设计控制了结构复杂度维度：观测量数量、隐变量数量（1–4）、观测到隐变量的方程格式（8 类）、隐变量到目标的计算格式（12 类）。\n关键结果是：加入 10-shot in-context examples 会在所有领域一致地把解题从知识推导推向示例拟合（位移是“普遍的”），但准确率变化呈现跨领域不一致：有的领域下降、有的上升、有的基本不变。作者据此提出三种 regime：由被替代策略（S_know）与替代策略（S_emp）的质量差距决定，解释了为什么单看平均准确率会得出互相矛盾的结论。\n（注：你提供的全文片段未包含各模型名称、每个领域的具体提升/下降数值与显著性检验细节；若需要逐项数字，需要补充论文后半部分的结果表与附录。）","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 25–35 分钟）：\n- 先看 Introduction 里对 failure mode of in-context learning in scientific settings: for tasks that require latent structure recovery—inferring hidden parameters from observations and then applying a known scientific law—adding in-context examples that are perfectly consistent with the underlying law can suppress the model’s retrieval and use of pretrained scientific knowledge. Instead of reinforcing formula-based derivation, demonstrations shift the model toward example-conditioned empirical fitting.","importance":"For pretraining research, the key implication is that ICL is not a neutral “extra information” channel; it can systematically change the computation the model chooses at inference time (retrieval+derivation vs. conditional function fitting). This complicates common evaluations that interpret k-shot gains as evidence of stronger scientific knowledge: the model may be fitting the in-context mapping rather than recalling and applying the governing equation. It also suggests an interaction with post-training: alignment procedures that reward user accommodation and pattern completion could further bias models toward example-driven strategies, potentially degrading performance when explicit law recall is required. Practically, it cautions against defaulting to demonstrations in scientific/engineering assistants without prompt designs that explicitly trigger equation recall and structured derivation.","technical_approach":"The authors propose a strategy-competition account. On latent structure recovery problems, an LLM can follow:\n(1) Knowledge-driven derivation (S_know): semantic cues trigger retrieval of a domain equation from pretrained memory; the model explicitly performs g(X)->Θ (latent extraction) and then h(Θ)->Y (target computation).\n(2) Example-driven fitting (S_emp): the model bypasses domain knowledge and approximates the end-to-end mapping f(X) directly by conditioning on k-shot demonstrations.\nTheir central claim is not that demonstrations combine with knowledge, but that demonstrations reweight these strategies, shifting probability mass from S_know to S_emp (“knowledge displacement”) across domains—even when demonstrations are generated by the same formula the model already knows. They argue that aggregate accuracy can mask this mechanism because S_emp can be worse, better, or similar to S_know depending on the domain and task conditions.","experiments":"They build a 60-task benchmark across five domains (Biology, Chemistry, Economics, Physics, Geoscience; 12 tasks each) with balanced structural difficulty. Tasks vary by number of observables, number of latent variables (1–4), observable-to-latent equation formats (8 types), and latent-to-target computation formats (12 types). Across ~6,000 trials and four model architectures, adding 10 in-context examples consistently induces the shift from knowledge-driven derivation to example-driven fitting in every domain. However, accuracy effects differ by domain—sometimes decreasing, sometimes increasing, sometimes flat—motivating a regime analysis based on the quality gap between the displaced and replacement strategies.\n(Note: the provided full-text excerpt does not include the detailed per-model/per-domain numeric results tables; relying on aggregate score deltas that hide bidirectional churn (improvements on some items, regressions on others).","importance":"This is evaluation methodology, but it matters for pretraining iteration and continual training because it gives a principled way to quantify regressions at the item level. A small net gain can mask substantial losses on previously high-confidence items. The RCI framing turns version-to-version drift into actionable diagnostics: which difficulty bands and domains reliably improved vs reliably deteriorated, under a fixed sampling policy.","technical_approach":"The paper adapts the clinical Reliable Change Index (RCI) to stochastic LLM evaluation. Each benchmark item is treated as the unit of change; repeated generations (K=10 at T=0.7) are treated as repeated measurements. For each item i and model m, accuracy is estimated as p(i,m) over K samples.\nReliability is estimated via repeated random split-halves of the K samples (5 vs 5), Spearman–Brown corrected; ICC is reported as a robustness check. Using reliability r_xx, they compute SEM = SD(p) * sqrt(1 - r_xx) per model, then S_diff = sqrt(SEM_v1^2 + SEM_v2^2) per version pair. Item-level RCI is (p(i,v2) - p(i,v1)) / S_diff, with |RCI| > 1.96 indicating reliable change (improvement or deterioration).\nA key practical point is that many items are at floor/ceiling, shrinking the analyzable set; the method therefore doubles as a benchmark quality diagnostic under the chosen sampling regime.","experiments":"They run 2,000 MMLU-Pro items (4 domains × 500 items; 10-way multiple choice) with K=10 samples per item per model at T=0.7, totaling 80,000 trials. Two within-family comparisons are tested: Llama-3-8B-Instruct → Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct (aggregate +1.6 points) and Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct → Qwen3-8B (non-thinking) (aggregate +2.8 points).\nOn the full benchmark, most items show no reliable change (79% for Llama, 72% for Qwen), but more than half the items are floor/ceiling. On the analyzable subset, churn is strongly bidirectional with large effect sizes: for Llama, 34% reliably improved and 28% reliably deteriorated; for Qwen, 47% improved and 39% deteriorated. Median |Δp| is reported around 0.50 (Llama) and 0.90 (Qwen). Difficulty-stratified results show asymmetric churn: low-accuracy items tend to improve while high-accuracy items tend to deteriorate; domain-level breakdown shows family-specific reversals.","reading_guide":"Read the Method section carefully: how reliability is estimated from K samples, how SEM and S_diff are constructed, and why this differs from negative-flip counting on single greedy runs. For each tested model, system prompts, task instructions and few-shot examples are optimized separately based on a small set of training samples, and evaluation is conducted with the model-specific optimized prompt. Existing sandboxes often rely on exact string matching, which mislabels correct programs for tasks with multiple valid outputs or floating-point tolerances, and they do not scale well under high-concurrency training workloads.","importance":"This is not a base pretraining paper, but it is relevant to code-model post-training and verifiable-reward pipelines. In RLVR, the verifier defines the reward. If correct programs are systematically rejected, the model receives biased gradients and training can become unstable. The paper is useful because it treats code execution infrastructure as part of the training signal, not just an evaluation utility.","technical_approach":"ScaleBox extends SandboxFusion with three main components. First, it adds automated special-judge generation and management, so tasks with multiple valid outputs or numeric tolerances can be checked with adaptive logic rather than exact string comparison. Second, it implements fine-grained parallel execution at both the problem-instance and unit-test levels, together with multi-node coordination. This is designed to use idle CPU resources for large volumes of code execution while accelerators handle model training. Third, it provides a configuration-driven evaluation suite for major code benchmarks, making execution settings and benchmark protocols easier to reproduce. Compared with simple exact-match sandboxes or statically deployed evaluators, the main contribution is higher verifier fidelity and better cluster-level throughput.","experiments":"The preliminary study analyzes 34,757 Python problems from PrimeIntellect. Using Qwen3-235B to identify tasks requiring special judges, the authors find that 14.57% of problems are incompatible with exact match. Among those, 78.26% allow multiple valid outputs and 21.74% require floating-point comparison with tolerance. More importantly, 59.01% of correct solutions for these tasks are falsely rejected by exact match. On AetherCode, the synthetic special judges reach over 84% per-solution precision. For efficiency, ScaleBox reports a 59% speedup over verl native execution and scales across multiple nodes. In RLVR experiments with Qwen3-8B, ScaleBox as the verification backend improves performance on LCB-V5 and LCB-V6 over heuristic-matching baselines, although the provided text does not include the full gain numbers.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20 to 30 minutes. Finally, check the RLVR setup: reward definition, sampling, training steps, benchmark splits, and failure cases. Readers focused only on base pretraining can skim the deployment dashboard details. the diagram with a blank image does not hurt—and can even improve—performance (the “Mirage” phenomenon).","importance":"Not a new pretraining objective paper, but a sharp evaluation/diagnosis contribution for multimodal pretraining and grounding: it shows how easily benchmarks get confounded by strong textual priors co-present in the prompt, and why “good scores” can be consistent with near-zero visual usage. For VLM and unified multimodal modeling, it argues for counterfactual evaluation (blank-image, identifier anonymization) and for training data/objectives that explicitly suppress header-driven shortcut learning; otherwise the model will internalize a bypass of the visual pathway. To rigorously assess whether models are truly generalizing or merely retrieving memorized templates, we introduce a strictly controlled program synthesis environment based on a domain-specific arithmetic grammar. By systematically enumerating and evaluating millions of unique programs, we construct inter","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-30T07:58:39+00:00","category_id":"coding_agent","cover_url":"/covers/2604_27551.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是“程序合成能不能做”，而是一个更基础也更难回答的问题：在训练语料完全可控、测试样本与训练集距离可度量的条件下，Transformer 在程序合成里到底是在泛化，还是只是在训练分布附近做局部插值。作者为此构造了一个严格受控的算术 DSL 环境，枚举数百万个程序，并把每个程序同时放到语法空间和语义空间里，从而能精确区分两类泛化：同一支持集内的数据密度迁移，以及超出训练支持集的外推。","importance":"对预训练研究的价值不在于它提出了新的大模型架构，而在于它把“泛化边界”这个平时很难测的东西做成了可操作实验对象。代码模型、算法学习、合成数据预训练都长期受数据污染和训练集不可见的困扰，这篇工作提供了一种更干净的分析范式：先把任务空间显式参数化，再讨论模型对分布密度、结构覆盖和支持集边界的响应。对基座研究员来说，最有用的结论有两个。第一，训练数据的覆盖方式比单纯从一个自然分布里多采样更关键，尤其是在结构化任务上。第二，扩大算力和模型规模能持续改善性能，但对超出语法支持集的外推帮助有限，提升更像对数线性而不是出现能力跃迁。这对“只靠 scale 是否足以学会算法泛化”给出了偏保守的证据。","technical_approach":"方法设计很克制，重点在可测性而不是任务复杂度。作者先定义一个一元标量算术程序的上下文无关文法，终结符包括 x、四则运算以及 sin/cos/exp/log/sqrt，抽象语法树的运算节点深度限制为 L=6，用穷举方式生成所有合法程序。随后做程序约简，去掉语义等价或无效表达式，得到可管理但仍然足够大的程序集合。\n\n核心创新是双流形表示。语法流形用结构相似度刻画程序树之间的距离，语义流形则通过程序在输入网格上的函数行为来定义距离。这样一来，作者不只是知道一个样本“没见过”，而是知道它在结构上离训练集多远、在行为上离训练集多远。基于这两个空间，作者构造了两类分布偏移：\n1. density generalization：测试样本仍落在训练支持集覆盖的区域内，但训练采样密度不同，考察模型能否从稠密区域推广到稀疏区域；\n2. support generalization：测试样本位于训练支持集之外，尤其是语法结构上超出训练覆盖，考察真正外推。\n\n训练数据选择是论文里最有信息量的部分。作者比较了多种采样策略，不只看自然频率采样，还显式做语法多样性采样、语义多样性采样，以及同时覆盖语法和语义的联合多样性采样。结论是，只优化单一维度的覆盖并不够，联合覆盖能显著改善 OOD 泛化。这一点和预训练里的数据配比、去重、覆盖率设计很像：不是样本越多越好，而是支持集的几何形状决定了模型能学到什么。","experiments":"关键结果主要有四类。\n\n第一，OOD 距离和性能之间存在清晰关系。随着测试样本在语法空间里离训练集越来越远，Transformer 的 pass@k 明显下降。论文强调语法距离比单纯语义距离更能决定失败模式，说明模型对程序结构分布的依赖很强。\n\n第二，训练集采样策略显著影响泛化。作者报告，若从单一分布采样，pass@1 大约在 10% 左右；若训练集同时在语法和语义空间上做多样化覆盖，pass@1 可提升到约 19%。这个提升幅度在受控环境里不小，因为测试分布是刻意隔离过的，不太可能来自模板记忆。\n\n第三，support generalization 依然很难。即便增加训练算力或模型规模，超出语法支持集后的性能提升仍然有限，趋势接近 log-linear，而不是在某个规模点突然跨过去。作者把这解释为 Transformer 更擅长局部插值和边界附近泛化，而不是系统性外推。\n\n第四，论文还做了不同 split 构造和距离定义下的稳健性检查，说明结论不是某一种切分方式偶然造成的。虽然给出的全文片段里没有完整参数表，但从引言可确认实验是在数百万枚举程序上进行，且比较重点是不同数据分布设计对 pass@1 / pass@k 的影响，而不是追求大模型绝对分数。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction 和 Methodology，抓住两个关键词：semantic manifold 和 syntactic manifold。只要理解作者如何把程序空间变成“可测距离的几何空间”，后面的实验就容易读。\n\n建议重点看三部分。第一，DSL 构造、枚举和 reduction 过程。这里决定了实验是否真的干净，尤其要看作者如何处理等价表达式、非法值和搜索空间爆炸。第二，train/test split 的定义方式。要确认 density shift 和 support shift 是否被严格区分，避免把“稀有样本”误当成“外推样本”。第三，采样策略对比图和 OOD 距离曲线，这部分最接近对预训练数据设计的启发。\n\n如果只花 20 到 30 分钟，建议看引言、任务定义图、主要结果图和结论。若要精读，值得深挖两个点：一是语法距离与语义距离哪个更预测失败；二是联合多样性采样为什么优于单维多样性，这和代码预训练里的 curriculum、mixture design、coverage-aware sampling 可以直接类比。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a more fundamental question than raw program-synthesis accuracy: under a fully controlled training distribution, when does a transformer genuinely generalize, and when is it only interpolating near memorized templates? To answer that, the authors build a transparent arithmetic DSL, enumerate millions of programs, and place each program in both a syntactic and a semantic metric space. This makes distribution shift measurable rather than anecdotal, and allows them to separate in-support density shift from true out-of-support extrapolation.","importance":"Its value for pretraining research is methodological. In code modeling and algorithmic reasoning, contamination and opaque corpora make it hard to tell whether gains come from real abstraction or retrieval from nearby examples. This work provides a clean experimental template: explicitly parameterize the task space, define distances to the training set, and study how performance degrades as coverage changes. Two conclusions matter for foundation-model researchers. First, coverage of the training support matters more than naive sampling from a single distribution, especially for structured tasks. Second, scaling helps, but only smoothly and only up to a point; it does not remove the extrapolation barrier once test programs move outside the syntactic support seen in training.","technical_approach":"$37","experiments":"$38","reading_guide":"$39","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"不是基座方法论文，但评测 setup 很干净；若你在做代码预训练或合成数据，重点看它怎样把 interpolation 和 extrapolation 拆开，别只盯最终分数。","en":"Not a foundation-model methods paper, but the setup is unusually clean; if you work on code pretraining or synthetic curricula, read it for how it separates interpolation from true extrapolation."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Friedrich Schiller University Jena","Friedrich Schiller UniversityJena"],"authors":["Henrik Voigt","Michael Habeck","Joachim Giesen"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["program-synthesis","generalization","distribution-shift","controlled-benchmark","transformers"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27551","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2604.27551","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2604.27319","title":"REBENCH: A Procedural, Fair-by-Construction Benchmark for LLMs on Stripped-Binary Types and Names (Extended Version)","abstract":"Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable progress in recent years, driving their adoption across a wide range of domains, including computer security. 的接口。对基座研究员来说，这里的启发是：交互环境中的数据质量，不只是轨迹是否成功，还包括任务定义是否 grounded、探索是否系统、状态转移是否覆盖关键能力边界。贡献量级上，它更像是 web-agent 方向的数据基础设施工作，但因为它直接触及训练数据分布的形成机制，所以比一般 benchmark 刷分更值得细读。","technical_approach":"$3a","experiments":"实验部分最关键的不是单一 benchmark 分数，而是作者如何证明“更全面的探索”确实转化成了“更好的训练数据”和“更强的站点模型”。从全文引言和方法描述可以确认，评估至少覆盖三层：网站探索覆盖度、任务与轨迹质量、以及用生成数据训练后的 agent / wLLM 表现。\n\n第一层，探索质量。作者将 AutoSurfer 与 homepage-based 方法和 random-walk 方法比较，核心指标应包括页面覆盖、动作覆盖、功能覆盖，以及冗余探索比例。论文的主张是 BFS + knowledge propagation 能发现更深层的网站功能，同时减少重复访问。静态元素系统遍历、动态元素代表性采样的组合，应该在 coverage 与 cost 之间取得更好的平衡。\n\n第二层，任务与轨迹质量。作者强调现有方法容易生成 hallucinated 或 ambiguous tasks，因此实验应包含人工或模型评估，检查任务是否 grounded、是否清晰、是否与真实网站能力一致，以及生成轨迹是否可执行、是否完整。AutoSurfer 的优势预期主要体现在任务更具体、前置条件更明确、轨迹成功率更高。\n\n第三层，也是最重要的一层，是训练价值。作者明确声称 AutoSurfer 生成的数据可以“meaningfully train a website-specific LLM”。这意味着他们不仅做了数据集展示，还训练了 wLLM，并在站内任务执行上与未做站点训练的模型或用其他数据生成器训练的模型比较。这里应重点关注：wLLM 的参数规模、训练 token 量或轨迹数、训练轮数、以及在 seen / unseen tasks 上的成功率提升。如果论文给出了小模型接近或超过更大通用模型的结果，这会是最有含金量的部分，因为它说明高质量站点数据可以部分替代通用规模优势。\n\n由于给出的全文片段在方法后半和实验表格处截断，无法安全复述具体数字。精读时应直接核对主实验表、coverage 统计图、任务质量人工评审表，以及 wLLM 训练设置。尤其要看作者是否报告了探索成本、每站点数据量、以及训练收益随数据规模变化的曲线；这些信息决定它对 pretraining / continual pretraining 的参考价值。","reading_guide":"这篇适合精读。建议先看 Introduction 和 Table 1，明确它相对 AgentTrek、Explorer、OS-Genesis、SynthAgent 分别补了哪一块短板。然后重点读方法部分的 BFS exploration 设计，尤其是 page queue、action trace 保留、knowledge propagation、静态/动态元素分治这几处。这里是整篇最有信息量的地方，因为它决定了后面任务合成为什么会更稳。\n\n接着看 task synthesis 部分，不要只看 prompt 模板，要看作者如何利用 exploration trajectory 约束任务表述。若只读摘要，很容易把它误解成“又一个自动生成 web trajectories 的系统”；全文真正有价值的是它把任务 grounding 建立在过程信息上，而不是终态信息上。\n\n实验部分优先看三类图表：coverage 对比、任务/轨迹质量评估、wLLM 训练结果。若时间有限，先确认它是否真的把更好的探索转化成了更好的训练收益；如果这条链条不成立，方法价值会下降很多。若要深挖，建议额外关注失败案例：BFS 是否会在登录态、分页、搜索、弹窗、异步加载页面上失效；动态内容采样是否会漏掉关键长尾功能；wLLM 的收益是否局限于单站点记忆而非可迁移能力。完整读一遍大约需要 60–90 分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a central bottleneck in training web agents: automatically generated web trajectories are often low quality because existing methods do not cover websites well, synthesize tasks that are weakly grounded, and produce unreliable execution traces. The authors decompose the issue into three linked failures: incomplete exploration, weak task discovery and formulation, and unstable trajectory generation built on top of that weak foundation. On unseen websites, agents often fail not because they cannot click a button, but because they never formed a usable internal map of the site’s deeper functionality. AutoSurfer aims to systematically traverse a website, connect pages, actions, capabilities, tasks, and trajectories into a coherent data-generation pipeline, and then test whether the resulting data can actually train a website-specific LLM, or wLLM.","importance":"$3b","technical_approach":"$3c","experiments":"$3d","reading_guide":"$3e","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看数据生成流程和覆盖率实验；它不会改变预训练方法论，但对交互轨迹数据的采集工程有参考价值。","en":"Read the data-generation pipeline and coverage experiments; it will not change pretraining methodology, but it is useful engineering signal for interactive trajectory collection."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Microsoft Research"],"authors":["Fazle Elahi Faisal","Qianhui Wu","Baolin Peng","Jianfeng Gao"],"notable_authors":["Jianfeng Gao","Baolin Peng"],"tags":["web-agent","trajectory-generation","agent-data","GUI-exploration"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27253","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2604.27253","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2604.27151","title":"Step-level Optimization for Efficient Computer-use Agents","abstract":"Computer-use agents provide a promising path toward general software automation because they can interact directly with arbitrary graphical user interfaces instead of relying on brittle, application-specific integrations. Despite recent advances in benchmark performance, strong computer-use agents remain expensive and slow in practice, since most systems invoke large multimodal models at nearly every interaction step. We argue that this uniform allocation of compute is fundamentally inefficient ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-29T19:59:36+00:00","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2604_27151.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 computer-use agent 在推理阶段的算力分配问题：现有 GUI agent 往往在每一步都调用同一个大多模态模型，导致长轨迹任务的成本、时延和单位经济性都很差。作者指出，GUI 轨迹的难度并不均匀，真正容易出错的往往集中在少数高风险时刻，主要表现为两类：一是 progress stall，即代理在局部状态里打转、重复等价操作却没有推进；二是 silent semantic drift，即动作在局部上看似合理，但已经偏离用户目标，后续步骤虽然自洽，最终任务仍会失败。核心问题不是如何再训练一个更强 agent，而是如何在不改动底层大模型的前提下，把“大模型每步在线”改成“按风险事件触发”。","importance":"对基座预训练本身的直接贡献有限，它更接近部署期控制与 agent inference orchestration，而不是新的预训练目标、数据配方或建模范式。但它有两个值得注意的启发。第一，长程交互任务中的错误分布高度稀疏且结构化，这说明未来如果要做 agent-oriented pretraining 或 mid-training，训练信号不一定要均匀铺在每一步，围绕关键转折点构造监督可能更高效。第二，这篇工作把“何时需要强模型”显式建模成一个可学习的风险预测问题，这对后续做 test-time compute allocation、分层 policy、甚至 pretrain 阶段的 curriculum 设计都有参考价值。贡献量级更像是一个扎实的系统方法：它改善了 cost-quality trade-off，但没有改变 web/GUI agent 的能力上限。","technical_approach":"方法是一个事件驱动的 step-level cascade。默认由小模型执行 GUI policy，只有在监控器判断风险升高时才升级到更强模型。控制器包含两个轻量监控器，输入都是最近若干步交互历史的滚动摘要，而不是完整长上下文。其一是 Stuck Monitor，用来判断当前轨迹是否处于退化执行状态，尤其是循环、无进展、重复点击等局部失败模式；其二是 Milestone Monitor，用来判断当前步骤是否完成了一个语义上有意义的阶段性检查点。作者的设计取舍很明确：如果每步都做强模型校验，成本太高；如果只在最终结果做校验，又太晚，无法纠正中途的语义漂移。Milestone 就是介于两者之间的稀疏监督单位。\n\n训练流程也偏工程化。作者先用小 agent 在多样网站和界面上收集交互轨迹，再让更强的 LLM 对短窗口片段打标签，标注哪些片段是健康推进、哪些是 stuck，以及哪些步骤构成 milestone。然后训练一个紧凑分类器，文中使用 BERT-family encoder，对短历史窗口输出校准后的风险分数。运行时采用带 hysteresis 的稳定控制策略和 bounded recovery budget：不是一次触发就频繁切换，而是要求风险持续存在才升级，并限制恢复阶段的大模型预算，避免控制器抖动和升级失控。方法的关键不是训练一个更强的动作模型，而是把大模型当作按需调用的恢复器和验证器。与 fixed-interval checking 相比，它利用了轨迹异质性；与纯启发式规则相比，它把 stuck 和 milestone 都做成了可学习信号，因此更容易迁移到不同 GUI 环境。","experiments":"从全文可见，实验重点不是追求绝对成功率，而是证明事件驱动升级比“全程大模型”与“固定间隔检查”更划算。作者在多步 GUI benchmark 上评估了任务成功率、强模型调用次数、wall-clock time 和美元成本。结论是：在接近 always-large agent 成功率的前提下，cascade 显著减少了大模型使用量，从而降低时延和推理成本。文中前言给出的现实背景数字是，真实多步 computer-use 任务常有 20–35 个交互步骤、数十万 token、十几分钟级运行时间，若每步都用 frontier model，单任务成本可超过 1 美元；这也是该方法试图解决的实际瓶颈。\n\n消融部分的主要信息有三点。第一，只做 stuck detection 可以改善局部恢复，但对 silent semantic drift 的帮助有限；只做 milestone verification 能抓住部分语义偏航，但对循环和卡死恢复不足；两者结合效果最好，说明它们覆盖的是不同失败模式。第二，事件驱动检查优于固定步长检查，因为固定间隔无法对齐真正的风险时刻，要么浪费预算，要么错过关键节点。第三，轻量监控器的信号质量已经足以支撑稳定路由，不需要再引入一个中等规模的复杂 controller。由于给出的全文片段没有完整表格，无法可靠摘录更细的绝对数值、阈值和各 benchmark 分项结果；精读时需要直接核对主表和附录中的 calibration / threshold sweep。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先看 Introduction 和方法总图，抓住两个失败模式：stall 与 semantic drift，以及两个监控器分别解决什么。然后看控制逻辑部分，重点理解为什么作者选 milestone 作为稀疏验证单位，而不是每 k 步检查一次。实验部分优先看三类对比：always-large、always-small、fixed-interval checking。再看 ablation，确认 stuck monitor 和 milestone monitor 的互补性。若时间有限，20–30 分钟足够把主要思想读清。\n\n真正值得留意的点有两个。第一，监控器训练标签来自强模型对短窗口的判别，这是一种把 frontier model 蒸馏成运行时控制信号的思路。第二，作者强调 hysteresis 和 recovery budget，这说明路由问题不是单步分类精度，而是闭环控制稳定性。做 agent 系统的人可以从这里学到，controller 的误报和漏报要放在轨迹层面看，而不是只看 step-level F1。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets inference-time compute allocation for computer-use agents. Current GUI agents typically invoke the same large multimodal model at every interaction step, which makes long-horizon tasks slow and expensive. The key observation is that GUI trajectories are not uniformly difficult. Failures cluster around a small number of high-risk moments, mainly in two forms: progress stalls, where the agent loops or repeats equivalent actions without changing the state, and silent semantic drift, where the agent keeps acting plausibly but has already deviated from the user’s intent. The problem is therefore not to build a stronger agent from scratch, but to replace always-on large-model inference with event-triggered escalation.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the strict sense. Its main contribution is deployment-time control for agent inference rather than a new training objective, data recipe, or modeling paradigm. Still, it offers two useful lessons for foundation-model research. First, errors in long interactive trajectories are sparse and structured, which suggests that future agent-oriented pretraining or mid-training may benefit from concentrating supervision around critical transition points rather than treating all steps equally. Second, the paper formulates “when to spend strong-model compute” as a learnable risk prediction problem. That framing is relevant to test-time compute allocation, hierarchical policies, and possibly curriculum design for interactive model training. The contribution is best understood as a solid systems improvement to the cost-quality trade-off, not as a change to the capability frontier.","technical_approach":"$3f","experiments":"$40","reading_guide":"$41","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"读方法和失败检测 ablation；若路由信号不偷看未来，这会是 GUI Agent 降成本的实用 prior。","en":"Read the method and failure-detection ablations; if the router does not leak future information, this is a useful prior for cheaper GUI agents."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Yale NLP Lab","University of North Carolina at Chapel Hill","Y Yale NLP Lab","CUniversity of North Carolina at Chapel Hill"],"authors":["Jinbiao Wei","Kangqi Ni","Yilun Zhao","Guo Gan","Arman Cohan"],"notable_authors":["Arman Cohan"],"tags":["GUI-agent","computer-use","compute-allocation","failure-detection","agent-efficiency"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27151","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2604.27151","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2604.27660","title":"From Context to Skills: Can Language Models Learn from Context Skillfully?","abstract":"Many real-world tasks require language models (LMs) to reason over complex contexts that exceed their parametric knowledge. However, constructing such skills for context learning scenarios faces two challenges: the prohibitive cost of manual skill annotation f","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-30T09:53:15+00:00","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2604_27660.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"上下文学习场景下，从长文本、高专业密度的上下文中提取可复用技能的现有方法，要么依赖成本极高的人工标注，要么需要外部反馈验证技能有效性，而多数上下文学习任务没有可自动获取的外部反馈信号，无法自动化生成适配特定上下文的技能。","importance":"验证了无需参数更新、仅通过多代理自博弈迭代生成自然语言技能的范式，可以作为增强模型上下文学习能力的补充方案，也为预训练阶段注入更通用的技能提取、自演进能力提供了方向参考。","technical_approach":"提出Ctx2Skill多代理自演进框架，无需人工标注和外部反馈。核心是挑战者、推理者、裁判三个代理构成的自博弈循环：挑战者基于当前上下文和技能集生成测试任务和评分规则，推理者用当前技能集尝试解题，裁判对比评分规则给出结果反馈；失败案例用于更新推理者的技能集，简单案例用于优化挑战者的任务生成策略，同时引入跨时间重放机制选择泛化性最优的技能集，避免博弈坍缩。生成的上下文专属技能可直接接入任意LM，提升同一上下文下的任务表现。","experiments":"在CL-Bench的4类上下文学习任务（领域知识推理、规则系统应用、流程任务执行、经验发现与仿真）上测试，适配后的技能可稳定提升多个骨干模型的解题率：GPT-4.1从11.1%提升到16.5%，GPT-5.1从21.2%提升到25.8%，GPT-5.2从18.2%提升到21.4%，增益可迁移到自博弈阶段未见过的任务。","reading_guide":"泛读重点关注第2章的自博弈框架设计和第4章的实验结果，预计阅读时间20分钟，可跳过具体的代理prompt设计细节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"In existing context learning scenarios, methods for extracting reusable skills from long, technically dense contexts either rely on high-cost manual annotation, or require external feedback to verify skill effectiveness. Most context learning tasks have no automatically obtainable external feedback signals, making automated generation of context-specific skills unfeasible.","importance":"It validates that the paradigm of iteratively generating natural language skills via multi-agent self-play without parameter updates can be a supplementary solution to enhance models' context learning capability, and provides reference for injecting more general skill extraction and self-evolution capabilities during pre-training.","technical_approach":"Propose Ctx2Skill, a multi-agent self-evolution framework that requires no human annotation or external feedback. Its core is a self-play loop of three agents: Challenger generates test tasks and scoring rubrics based on current context and skill set, Reasoner attempts to solve tasks with its current skill set, and Judge provides performance feedback against the rubrics. Failed cases are used to update the Reasoner's skill set, while easy cases optimize the Challenger's task generation strategy. A cross-time replay mechanism is introduced to select the most generalizable skill set and avoid adversarial collapse. The generated context-specific skills can be directly plugged into any LM to improve performance on tasks under the same context.","experiments":"Evaluated on 4 context learning tasks from CL-Bench (domain knowledge reasoning, rule system application, procedural task execution, empirical discovery & simulation). The adapted skills consistently improve solution rates across multiple backbone models: GPT-4.1 from 11.1% to 16.5%, GPT-5.1 from 21.2% to 25.8%, GPT-5.2 from 18.2% to 21.4%, and the gains transfer to tasks unseen during the self-play phase.","reading_guide":"Skim, focus on the self-play framework design in Section 2 and experimental results in Section 4. We present a controlled comparison showing that for procedural tasks, this architecture is dominated by a simpler alternative: putting the entire procedure in the system prompt and letting the model self-orchestrate. Across three domains -- travel booking (14 nodes), Zoom technical support ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-30T14:07:37+00:00","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2604_27891.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"核心问题是：对有明确流程图的程序性对话任务，外部 agent 编排是否真的比把完整流程放进系统提示词更可靠。论文把问题限定在 intake、诊断、信息收集、决策分流、终止状态这类客服式流程，而不是开放式工具使用或长期任务规划。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接贡献有限，但对理解长上下文指令遵循和 agent 系统设计有参考意义。结果说明，当前强模型已经能在上下文内维护相当复杂的流程状态；外部编排把流程拆成局部节点后，反而可能破坏模型的全局推理和自然对话连续性。这个结论对基座评测也有启发：流程遵循能力不只取决于 agent 框架，也取决于模型在长系统提示中解析结构化程序、维持隐式状态、处理循环和分支的能力。","technical_approach":"实验设计较干净。两组系统使用同一个模型、同一套流程图、同一批场景和同一个用户模拟器。LangGraph 组把每个流程节点映射为 graph node，到达 agent 节点时只注入该节点的 prompt；遇到 decision hub 时额外调用 LLM 根据对话历史选择边。in-context 组则把完整流程图序列化进系统提示词，包括节点、边、条件和终止状态，由模型自行决定下一步。三类任务覆盖不同复杂度：travel booking 和 Zoom support 各 14 个节点、3 个决策点；insurance claims 有 55 个节点、6 个决策点，并包含多阶段循环和嵌套分支。方法的关键差异不是模型能力，而是状态和路由放在外部框架里，还是放在模型上下文里。","experiments":"每个领域每种条件 200 段对话，共 1200 段。评估使用 LLM-as-judge，在五个质量维度上打分。in-context 组在三个领域、所有指标上都高于 LangGraph 组：整体分数范围为 4.53–5.00，而 LangGraph 为 4.17–4.84。效率上，LangGraph 因为路由调用带来 1.2–1.7 倍 LLM 调用开销。失败率差异也明显：编排组在不同领域有 9–24% 对话失败，in-context 组为 0.5–11.5%。失败分析把问题归为三类：决策点路由错误、节点化提示导致推理被切碎、对话自然度受限。需要注意，评估依赖模拟用户和 LLM judge，且模型是前沿闭源模型；结论更适合说明强模型上的流程编排取舍，不宜直接外推到弱模型、强工具依赖任务或需要可审计状态机的场景。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 20 分钟。先看 Method 中两种系统的对照设计，确认变量是否隔离干净；再看三个 workflow 的复杂度设定，尤其是 55 节点保险理赔流程，因为它决定了结论能否覆盖较大流程图。结果部分重点看质量分数、调用次数和失败率三张结果表或图。附录 H 的系统提示词和附录 F 的节点提示值得扫一遍，可以判断 in-context 组是否从更完整的全局信息中获益，以及 LangGraph 组是否因为实现方式受到不公平限制。对预训练方向，最值得带走的是：长上下文程序执行能力可以作为模型能力评测项，而不是只把它看成 agent 工程问题。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether an external agent orchestrator is actually useful for procedural conversations with an explicit workflow, or whether a strong model can do better when the full procedure is placed in the system prompt. The scope is deliberately narrow: customer-service style workflows with intake, routing, loops, resolution, and terminal states, not open-ended autonomous agents.","importance":"The contribution is not a pretraining method, but it is relevant to how foundation models expose procedural competence at inference time. The main signal is that strong long-context models can maintain workflow state internally when the entire procedure is visible. External orchestration may remove global context, add routing errors, and make the conversation less coherent. For model research, this suggests that structured instruction following, implicit state tracking, and branch execution inside long prompts deserve to be evaluated as model capabilities, not only as framework behavior.","technical_approach":"The comparison is controlled around a single architectural choice. Both conditions use the same model, the same workflows, the same scenarios, and the same simulated users. In the LangGraph condition, each workflow node becomes a graph node; agent nodes inject only the local node prompt, and decision hubs trigger an additional LLM call to choose the outgoing edge from the conversation history. In the in-context condition, the entire workflow graph is serialized into the system prompt, including nodes, edges, conditions, and terminal states, and the model self-routes. The three domains span increasing complexity: travel booking and Zoom technical support have 14 nodes and 3 decision hubs each; insurance claims processing has 55 nodes, 6 decision hubs, multiple phases, loops, and nested dependencies.","experiments":"The study evaluates 200 conversations per condition per domain, for 1,200 conversations in total. Conversations are scored by an LLM judge on five quality criteria. The in-context setup scores 4.53–5.00 on a 5-point scale, while the LangGraph setup scores 4.17–4.84. The orchestrated setup also needs 1.2–1.7 times more LLM calls because decision hubs require routing calls. Failure rates are higher under orchestration: 9–24% across domains, compared with 0.5–11.5% for the in-context baseline. The reported failure modes are routing mistakes at decision hubs, fragmented reasoning caused by local node prompts, and less natural conversation flow. The main caveat is that the evaluation depends on simulated users and LLM-as-judge scoring, and the model is a strong closed model; the result should not be assumed to hold for weaker models, heavy tool-use settings, or systems that require auditable external state.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20 minutes. Appendix H and Appendix F are worth a quick pass because they reveal how much global information the in-context model receives and how constrained the LangGraph node prompts are.
